**The two reviewers disagree on one consequence, and the disagreement is live.** [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) holds that the defect-cubic branch caps the chain at $n^{-3/2}$ **even with $\sup\lvert P\rvert=O(1)$**, so the exponent is pinned by $(c_p=\Theta(1/n),\lVert A''\rVert=O(n))$ alone and [I31](../plan.md) provably cannot move it. [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md) holds that this is **conditional on an unpriced fact**: the analyticity radius used throughout is direction-blind, being $\Theta(\min(1,g_n/\lVert H\rVert_{op}))$ along $H$, and where $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\asymp g_n$ the disc is $\Theta(1)$ and $\lVert A''\rVert=O(1)$, on such directions the defect-tail branch stops capping and the cubic branch $c_p/\lVert P\rVert_\infty=\Theta(n^{-1})$ takes over.

> **DECIDED BY [LEMMA L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects), FOR [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md).** *On the summand carrying the margin, $\lVert H\rVert_{op}=O(n^{-1/2})=o(1)$, which is the test [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md) set. So $\lVert A''\rVert=O(1)$ rather than $O(n)$, the input [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) named as its own point of failure, the defect branch stops capping, and the radius below becomes a lower bound with an exponent of room in it rather than an equality.* **[I31](../plan.md) buys an exponent, not a constant.**

#### Lemma L146, the operator norm on the margin summand, and the direction-blind radius it corrects

*Derived 2026-09-03 to decide the [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md)/[r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) disagreement recorded in [Lemma L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports). **Gate 2: [r448](../reviews/REVIEW_r448.md) and [r449](../reviews/REVIEW_r449.md), independently on identical briefs, REFUTED the derivation and CONFIRMED the order-level conclusion.** Every structural statement below was wrong in the first version; the two errors moved numerator and denominator of $\lVert H\rVert_{op}/\lVert H\rVert_F$ by the same order, so the headline survived them.*

**Setting.** At $W^*=S^m\times S^m$, [Lemma L27](nondegeneracy.md) puts the off-orbit margin maximum on the two $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ summands, and $L_e$ acts there as the **scalar** $\lambda_m=(m-2)/(2(m-1))$. *So the margin is constant across the summand and only $\lVert H\rVert_{op}$ varies over it.* Both reviewers confirmed the margin is **exactly** [Lemma L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports)'s $c_p$: $3\theta_0(\tfrac12-\lambda_m)=3\theta_0/(n-2)$.

> **THE EMBEDDING, CORRECTED.** $\mathrm{Ric}(h\owedge g_q)=m\,h\ne0$, so $h\owedge g_q$ is **not a Weyl tensor** and the projection term survives. Against the record's own projector ($\mathrm{KN}(h)=\tfrac12(h\owedge g)$, [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md)), with $\alpha=\tfrac{m-2}{2(m-1)}$ and $\beta=\tfrac m{2(m-1)}$, the Weyl image is diagonal on coordinate $2$-planes with
> $$\alpha\,h_a\ \text{ on }e_a\wedge e_b,\ a\in B_1,\ b\in B_2;\qquad -\beta\,(h_a+h_{a'})\ \text{ on }e_a\wedge e_{a'},\ a,a'\in B_1;\qquad 0\ \text{ inside }B_2.$$
> *An earlier version of this lemma took the eigenvalues to be $h_a$ on the cross planes and **zero inside both blocks**, which is $h\owedge g_q$ before the projection. **The in-block planes it discarded are the ones that dominate the operator norm.***

> **THE IN-BLOCK BRANCH DOMINATES, AND THE ARGUMENT IS THIS.** Tracelessness forces $\max_{a\ne a'}\lvert h_a+h_{a'}\rvert\ge\tfrac{m-2}{m-1}\max_a\lvert h_a\rvert$, *sharp at the spike, and not obvious: $h=(3,-1,-1,-1)$ has pair-maximum $2$ against entry-maximum $3$*, while $\beta/\alpha=\tfrac m{m-2}$ covers that ratio with room. **The clause was asserted without argument until [ROUND_L146](../verification/ROUND_L146.md); two verifiers derived this proof independently.** *Machine-checked: `pair_ge`, `inblock_dominates` and their combination `inblock_wins` in [L146Dom.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Results/L146Dom.lean).*

> **THIS PRICES ONE COPY. ON THE JOINT TWO-COPY EIGENSPACE THE EQUALITY CLAUSE IS FALSE.** A pair $(h,k)$ carries a **third, cross-plane branch** $\alpha(h_a+k_b)$ that no single $h$ produces: at $m=5$, $h=k=(1,0,0,0,-1)$ gives $\tfrac34$ against the formula's $\tfrac58$, and at $m=6$, $h=k=(1,-\tfrac15,\dots)$ gives $\tfrac45$ against $\tfrac{12}{25}$. **The supremum is unchanged**, the cross branch caps at $\sqrt{m-2}/m<1/\sqrt{m-1}$, since $(m-1)(m-2)<m^2$, *so [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s use of this bound on the joint eigenspace is safe, and the line licensing it lives here rather than being assumed.* *Machine-checked: the branch values `cross5`, `inblock5`, `cross6`, `inblock6`, `traceless`, `cross_exceeds`, and `cross_cap`, the inequality $\sqrt{m-2}/m<1/\sqrt{m-1}$, in [L146Dom.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Results/L146Dom.lean); that the cross branch caps at $\sqrt{m-2}/m$ is by reading.*

**Diagonality does survive**, both Kulkarni–Nomizu factors being diagonal in the same frame, and that is the claim doing real work, since it gives $v=0$ and so identifies the summand with the $u=0$ branch that [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md) and [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) each asserted and neither proved.

> **LEMMA L146.** *For $H$ the Weyl image of a **single** $h\in S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ with $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$, $n=2m$ and $m\ge3$,*

> **HYPOTHESES, WRITTEN INTO THE STATEMENT WHERE A CITATION READS THEM.** *$m\ge5$, the word "margin" in the title needs it, and at $m=3$ the [L27](nondegeneracy.md) case cited is the excluded $(3,3)$ one. $\ker W^*=0$. The frame chosen **after** $h$. And $h$ ranges over the **joint** $\lambda_m$-eigenspace, both $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ copies together, at every norm*, **not one copy at unit norm, which is what the box said while all three consumers used the joint form.** *The extension was true and argued beside this statement, in a neighbour's set-up and in a review, and never inside it: [L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) received exactly this repair and this lemma did not. Three verifiers, [ROUND_L146-r2](../verification/ROUND_L146r2.md).*
> $$\lVert H\rVert_F^2=\frac{m(m-2)}{2(m-1)}\lVert h\rVert^2,\qquad \lVert H\rVert_{op}=\frac m{2(m-1)}\max_{a\ne a'}\lvert h_a+h_{a'}\rvert,$$
> *the in-block branch always winning, with supremum $1/\sqrt{m-1}$ attained at the pair-concentrated $h$. **In order, $\lVert H\rVert_{op}$ ranges over $[\Theta(n^{-1}),\Theta(n^{-1/2})]$.***

*The extremal problem is $\max_{a\ne a'}\lvert h_a+h_{a'}\rvert$, not $\max_a\lvert h_a\rvert$, and reported the supremum as $\sqrt{m-1}/m$, too small by $m/(m-1)$, with intervals disjoint from the truth at $m=3$. **The two lower bounds on file are not in conflict**: $1/(m-1)$ is a proven floor and $\approx\sqrt2/m$ an attained value, so they bracket one infimum rather than contradict, *recorded as a disagreement until [ROUND_L146](../verification/ROUND_L146.md), where two verifiers resolved it independently.* **And the even split is beaten only up to $m=6$**, the spike witness winning exactly when $m^2-8m+8<0$; at $m\ge7$ that witness loses and the clause has no proof, though a different family reaches $m=8$. *Both bounds are $\Theta(n^{-1})$, so the order is untouched either way.*

> **THE ORDER-LEVEL CONCLUSION SURVIVES THE CORRECTED EMBEDDING.** Every $H$ in the summand has $\lVert H\rVert_{op}=O(n^{-1/2})$, so the analyticity disc $g_n/\lVert H\rVert_{op}$ is $\Omega(n^{-1/2})$ throughout, **a factor $\sqrt n$ larger than the direction-blind $g_n=\Theta(n^{-1})$** that [Lemma L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports) uses. *This holds for $m\ge5$, where $\lambda_m>\mu$ puts the margin on this summand; $n\ge12$ is inside that.*

> **AND IT BEARS ON THE [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md)/[r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) SPLIT WITHOUT SETTLING IT.** *An earlier version of this entry claimed it settled the split for r446. It meets only the weaker $o(1)$ wording of [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md)'s test; the mechanism r446 names needs $\lVert H\rVert_{op}=O(g_n)$, which this does not give ([r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md), [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md)).*

> **AND [r448](../reviews/REVIEW_r448.md) AND [r449](../reviews/REVIEW_r449.md) CONTRADICT EACH OTHER HERE BY $\sqrt n$, WHICH THIS ENTRY ORIGINALLY HID.** r448 derives $M=O(\rho)$ and so $\lVert A''\rVert=O(n^{1/2})$; r449 holds $M=O(g_n)$ fixed and so gets $O(1)$. *The two coincide at $\rho=\Theta(g_n)$, which is why the difference was invisible.* **[r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) and [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) both resolve it for r448**: a Cauchy supremum taken over a larger disc is larger, and $x\mapsto x_-$ is $1$-Lipschitz, so $M=O(\rho)$.

> **AND [LEMMA L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) DISPLACES THIS LEMMA ENTIRELY.** *The defect is identically zero on this summand, so the operator norm computed here prices a quantity that is not present.* **What it establishes about the ten-summand infimum is nothing; the question lives on the mixed directions.**

**A screening note worth keeping.** A screen reporting $\max\lVert H\rVert_{op}/g_n$ at five dimensions. It re-evaluated the lemma's own closed form, built no tensor, and **could not have caught the embedding error by any outcome**, a screen that cannot fail is not a screen. [r448](../reviews/REVIEW_r448.md)'s, which constructed Kulkarni–Nomizu tensors and checked Bianchi and $\mathrm{Ric}$ before measuring, is what the word is for.

#### Lemma L147, the tube radius tracks $\sup\lvert P\rvert$ on $\beta\in[0,\tfrac12]$, and the route ends at $\Theta(n^{-1})$

*Derived 2026-09-03 by re-running [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md)'s chain. **Gate 2: [r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) and [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md), independently on identical briefs, REFUTED the mechanism and the headline and CONFIRMED the inequality as an order.** Every reason the first version gave was wrong; the two values survive.*

> **LEMMA L147.** *At $W^*=S^m\times S^m$ with $n=2m\ge12$, over directions $H$ with $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$, $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le1$, $H\perp W^*$ and $H\perp T_{W^*}\mathcal O$, **all four used by name in the proof and none previously in this box**, and with $\sup\lvert P\rvert=\Theta(n^\beta)$,*
> $$r_n\;\gtrsim\;n^{-3/2}\qquad\text{on }\beta\in[0,\tfrac12],$$
> *and the route is capped at $\Theta(n^{-1})$. **$\beta=\tfrac12$ gives $n^{-3/2}$ and is confirmed, pinned by the $P$-side branch alone. $\beta=0$ gives $n^{-1}$ and is **owed**, its constant unpinned and its joint minimisation not on file.** *The clause is $n^{-1-\beta}$ read at the left endpoint, so it stands or falls with the debt box below rather than independently of it.*

**The admissible range is a closed interval, and that is what makes the ceiling question empty.** $\sup\lvert P\rvert\ge P(W^*)=\theta_0(n)\uparrow\sqrt2$ forces $\beta\ge0$; [Lemma L143](bounds.md) forces $\beta\le\tfrac12$. *So the radius lives in $[n^{-3/2},n^{-1}]$ and there is no region in which a ceiling above $n^{-1}$ could bind.* **The first version claimed "no ceiling", which is false four separate ways**, the $P$-side branch caps itself at $c_p/\theta_0\le3/(n-2)$; the corrected quartic branch is $\Theta(n^{-1})$; the validity cap $r_n\le\rho/2$ is $O(g_n)$ on the directions where $\rho=\Theta(g_n)$; and $\beta\ge0$ empties the region regardless.

> **THE SUBSTITUTION $\lVert A''\rVert=O(1)$ IS WRONG BY $\sqrt n$, AND BOTH REVIEWERS DERIVED THE SAME REPLACEMENT.** $M=\sup_{\lvert z\rvert=\rho}\lVert A\rVert$ is a supremum **over the disc**, so it grows with it: $x\mapsto x_-$ is $1$-Lipschitz in Frobenius norm and $\lVert\mathrm{Alt}\rVert\le1$, giving $M=O(\rho)$ and hence
> $$\lVert A''\rVert=O(M/\rho^2)=O(1/\rho)=O(n^{1/2})\quad\text{on the margin summand},$$
> *which returns the record's own $O(n)$ at $\rho=\Theta(g_n)$, as it must.* **$O(1)$ would need $\lVert H\rVert_{op}=O(g_n)$, which is [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md)'s actual mechanism; [Lemma L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) meets only the weaker $o(1)$ wording of that test.*

> **AND IT WAS A QUANTIFIER ERROR.** [Lemma L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) bounds one summand; the chain's $\lVert A''\rVert$ ranges over **eight** of [Lemma L27](nondegeneracy.md)'s ten, $\langle W\rangle$ and the orbit summand are removed exactly by $H\perp W^*$ and $H\perp T_{W^*}\mathcal O$, *which is not bookkeeping: at ten the margin floor would be $-\tfrac32\theta_0$.* *A single mixed direction with $u\asymp n^{-1/2}$, margin $\Theta(1/n)$ and $\lVert A''\rVert=\Theta(n)$ restores $n^{-3/2}$ at every $\beta$.* **Both reviewers closed the sweep the lemma owed**: $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le\Theta(n^{-1/2})+w$ by the triangle inequality and $c(H)\gtrsim c_p+w^2$ since $L_e$ is scalar on each summand, uniformly in $w\in[0,1]$. It returns these values and the $\Theta(n^{-1})$ ceiling.

> **HYPOTHESES, AND THE DEBT.** *Base point $W^*=S^m\times S^m$; $n=2m$ even; $n\ge12$, which the imports force through $n^2-12n+8>0$; and $\ker W^*=0$, **all four used and none stated until [ROUND_L147](../verification/ROUND_L147.md).*** The kernel one has a named witness: $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ is Morse–Bott nondegenerate with $\dim\ker W=3$, so $g_n$, $\rho$, $M$ and branches 2–3 **do not exist there**.
>
> **The $\Theta(n^{-1})$ ceiling is proved**, by $c_p/S\le c_p/\theta_0=3/(n-2)$ alone, exact and unconditional in $\beta$. *Its reason (b) is unproven and false where read: it needs a **lower** bound on $\lVert A''\rVert$, and [L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) sets that to zero on the very summand.*
>
> **`HYPOTHESIS-DEBT`, RE-OPENED: THE RADIUS $r_n\gtrsim n^{-1-\beta}$ IS OWED, NOT REFUTED.** *A refutation was recorded here and is withdrawn: **every branch of the $\min$ is a lower bound on $r(H)$, so exhibiting a smaller lower bound falsifies nothing**, and [L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports) says in terms that the chain cannot yield an upper bound at all.* **The arithmetic behind it was also mixed**: it read branch 1's numerator at $w\asymp n^{-1/2}$ and its coefficient $a$ at $w=\Theta(1)$, where in fact $u^*=(\sqrt{S^2+a^2c_p}-S)/a\le\sqrt{c_p}=\Theta(n^{-1/2})$. *Three verifiers on identical briefs, [ROUND_L147-r2](../verification/ROUND_L147r2.md), unanimously.* **What survives from that attempt is [ROUND_L149-F](../verification/ROUND_L149F.md)'s F12**, $f'(0)<0$, so $u=0$ is not branch 1's minimum, **and the inference drawn from it does not.** *A prior review's A1 is named as paying this debt and has not been read against it.*

**The chain has two branches, not three.** $f(u)=(c_p+u^2)/(S+au)$ has $f'(0)=-ac_p/S^2<0$, so $u=0$ is **never** its minimum; the minimum is interior at $u^*=(\sqrt{S^2+a^2c_p}-S)/a$ with $f(u^*)=2u^*/a=\Theta(\min(c_p/S,\sqrt{c_p}/a))$, at every $\beta$ and every $a$. *The first version argued the cubic branch collapses to $u=0$ because the denominator is $S+u$, a reason that never uses $a$, and which would erase [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md)'s confirmed $n^{-3/2}$ cap.* **So the cubic branch always shadows the quartic, and "both defect branches moved out of the way" is impossible in principle.**

**$\gtrsim$, not $\Theta$.** The chain is an inequality, [Lemma L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports) withdrew its own equality reading, and $\sup\lvert P\rvert$ is bracketed rather than known, so $\beta$ is not an exponent. *The first version wrote $\Theta$, the exact error L145 step 4 had been corrected for two entries earlier.*

#### Lemma L148, the defect vanishes identically on the margin summand

> **HYPOTHESES AND SCOPE, WRITTEN IN AFTER [ROUND_L148](../verification/ROUND_L148.md).** *Base point $W^*=S^m\times S^m$, $n=2m$, $m\ge3$, and $m\ge5$ for the word "margin" in the title, since $\lambda_m-\mu=\tfrac{m-4}{2(m-1)}$ ties at $m=4$ and at $m=3$ the margin sits on $\Lambda^2\!\otimes\!\Lambda^2$ instead.* **The frame is chosen after $H$**: "$W^*$ and $H$ simultaneously diagonal on coordinate $2$-planes" is **false in a fixed frame**, $h_{12}=1$ gives $H_{1424}\ne0$ at every $m\ge3$, **the coefficient being $\alpha h_{12}$ only at $m=3$**, where index $4$ is the first index of $B_2$; at every $m\ge4$ index $4$ sits inside $B_1$ and the component is $-\beta h_{12}$, *$-\tfrac35$ against $\tfrac25$ at $m=6$. The non-vanishing it witnesses survives either coefficient, and $\beta>\alpha$ strengthens it*, and true per direction, by $W^*$'s $O(m)\times O(m)$-invariance together with $O(n)$-equivariance of $\mathrm{Alt}$ and $x\mapsto x_-$. *That sentence was absent.* **And the vanishing holds on both $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ copies**, $h$ on $V_1$ and $k$ on $V_2$ diagonalising in one frame, *written singular, used plural by every citation, which is the slip that cost [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) a factor of $n$.*
>
> **WHAT IS ABSENT AT $w=0$ IS THE TWO DEFECT *TERMS*, NOT TWO BRANCHES.** Branch 1 is present there and equals $c_p/S\le3/(n-2)$, *it is the branch that proves the route's $\Theta(n^{-1})$ ceiling.* Only branch 2 goes unbounded.
>
> **The conclusion is wider than the entry claims**: $\mathrm{Alt}(W(t)_-)=0$ for **every real $t$**, past every eigenvalue crossing, using no spectral gap, no analyticity, no $\ker W^*=0$ and no $n\ge12$, *so this is the one link in the tube chain that survives a kernel, and it asserts nothing at $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$, whose $\delta^2=\tfrac19\ne0$ shows contrapositively that it is coordinate-diagonal in no frame.*

*Found by [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) while attacking [Lemma L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1). **Gate 2 discharged by [ROUND_L148](../verification/ROUND_L148.md); the corrected text re-audited as [ROUND_L148-r2](../verification/ROUND_L148r2.md).***

> **LEMMA L148.** *Let $m\ge3$ and $W^*=S^m\times S^m$ with $n=2m$, and let $H$ lie in the joint $\lambda_m$-eigenspace $\Sigma$, **both** $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ copies, of dimension $(m+2)(m-1)$, with the diagonalising frame chosen **after** $H$. Then $W^*$ and $H$ are simultaneously diagonal on coordinate $2$-planes, hence so is $W(t)=W^*+tH$ and so is $W(t)_-$; and since $\mathrm{Alt}$ lands in $\Lambda^4$, whose components need four distinct indices, a coordinate-diagonal operator has none. **Therefore $\mathrm{Alt}(W(t)_-)=0$ for every real $t$, an exact algebraic identity on the affine subspace $W^*+\Sigma$, using no gap, no analyticity, no $\ker W^*=0$ and no smallness of $t$.** The defect is identically zero along the whole segment, **and the branch that vanishes there is the second one alone**: at $w=0$ the first branch is $c_p/S\le3/(n-2)$, finite and positive, and it is what proves the route's ceiling.*

> **THE SCOPE CORRECTIONS ARE IN THE STATEMENT ABOVE, WHICH IS WHERE A CITATION READS THEM.** *They had been written into the boxes around it and not into it, so the citable sentence still carried a singular copy, a fixed frame, no dimension floor and the refuted "both branches" clause, **the clause its own scope box denied four lines earlier**. Three verifiers on identical briefs, [ROUND_L148-r2](../verification/ROUND_L148r2.md).* **$\Sigma$ is the full $\lambda_m$-eigenspace only for $m\ge5$**: at $m=4$ the eigenspace strictly contains it, picking up $\Lambda^2\otimes\Lambda^2$, and at $m=3$ the margin is exactly zero, $\mu=\tfrac12$ tying the orbit at [L27](nondegeneracy.md)'s degenerate $S^3\times S^3$. *The word "margin" therefore carries $m\ge5$.*

> **THE COMPLEX-$z$ EXTENSION IS DISCHARGED, AND IT WAS ALREADY ON THE RECORD.** *The frame is fixed by $H$ alone, so it does not depend on $z$; $W(z)=W^*+zH$ is diagonal in it with complex entries; the resolvent of a diagonal operator is diagonal, so the Riesz projection is too, entry by entry; and $\mathrm{Alt}$ is a fixed rational-coefficient index formula, extending $\mathbb C$-linearly and killing a coordinate-diagonal operator over any field.* **The vanishing holds term by term in the decomposition of $A$, and uniformly in $\alpha$, the argument never using which entries $\Gamma$ selects.** *On $\Sigma$ the operator is diagonal in a **real orthonormal** basis, hence normal over $\mathbb C$ with orthogonal Riesz projections, so no complex-symmetric difficulty arises.* **Gate 2: [r727](../reviews/REVIEW_r727.md) and [r728](../reviews/REVIEW_r728.md), identical briefs, both confirming every step and both refuting its novelty**, [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) states it where L148 was found, and [v51](../verification/VERIFY_v51.md) writes it out in the paragraph that raised this debt.

> **AND THE CONSUMER NEEDS NO COMPLEX STATEMENT AT ALL.** *L148 gives $A(t)=0$ at every real $t$, so $A$ restricted to the reals is the zero function and [L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1)'s $\lVert A''\rVert=0$ follows with no disc, no contour and no gap*, **none of the hypotheses the complex route imports, and none that this lemma is on record for not needing.**

> **THE LIVE COMPLEXIFICATION GAP IS ELSEWHERE, AND IT IS NOT THIS ONE.** *[L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1) justifies $M=O(\rho)$ by "$x\mapsto x_-$ is 1-Lipschitz in Frobenius norm", **the source states that fact *on Hermitian operators* and those three words are dropped**, while the Cauchy supremum runs over $\lvert z\rvert=\rho$ where $W^*+zH$ is complex symmetric and $x\mapsto x_-$ has no functional calculus.* **The conclusion is not in doubt and the written reason is: the same review gives a resolvent derivation that is complex-valid, and the entry cited the wrong one of its two.** *Repairing it costs [L156](#lemma-l156-the-contour-constant-corrected-and-its-optimum-attained-at-the-equidistant-contour)'s contour constant, which is unbounded as $\gamma\uparrow d$.* **A second, separate one:** the two discs differ, [L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects)'s analyticity disc is at $\gamma=1$, L156's excluded endpoint, against $\gamma^*=D/4$, a factor $\tfrac{20}{11}$ in radius and $3.31$ in the Cauchy $\rho^2$; **on the larger disc the enclosed cluster reaches $0$ and the integrand stops being the defect.**

> **THIS IS STRONGER THAN [LEMMA L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) AND IT DISPLACES IT.** *A bound on $\lVert A''\rVert$ over a summand where $A\equiv0$ prices a quantity that is not there.* **The question lives on the mixed directions**, which neither L146 nor L147 prices, and which is where a successor must work.

#### Lemma L149, no direction is both low-margin and high-operator-norm, so the bound is uniform

*Derived 2026-09-03 as [I33](../plan.md), completing the sweep [r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) and [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) each began. **Gate 2: [r452](../reviews/REVIEW_r452.md) and [r453](../reviews/REVIEW_r453.md), independently on identical briefs, returned PLAUSIBLE with the margin step REFUTED as stated and repairable.** It removes the quantifier error that refuted [Lemma L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1)'s mechanism: the estimate is over **all** of $T_{W^*}S\cap T_{W^*}\mathcal O^\perp$.*

> **THE SUBSTANCE, STATED AS BOTH REVIEWERS STATE IT.** *No direction is both low-margin and high-operator-norm*, and that single fact is the sweep:
> $$\lVert A''\rVert=\Theta(n)\ \Rightarrow\ \lVert H\rVert_{op}=\Theta(1)\ \Rightarrow\ w=\Theta(1)\ \Rightarrow\ c(H)=\Theta(1).$$
> **So [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md)'s counterexample direction, margin $\Theta(1/n)$ with $\lVert A''\rVert=\Theta(n)$, does not exist.** *An earlier version of this entry called it the minimiser, which is weaker and was wrong: margin $\Theta(1/n)$ forces $w\lesssim n^{-1/2}$, hence $\lVert A''\rVert=O(n^{1/2})$, and the branch returns to $\Theta(n^{-1})$.*

**Set-up.** At $p=q=m$ there are **two** $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ summands, both carrying $\lambda_m$, the swap of the factors fixes $W^*$ and commutes with $L_e$. *Write $H=H_s+H_r$ with $H_s$ in the **joint** $\lambda_m$-eigenspace, both copies, and $w:=\lVert H_r\rVert$ the weight outside **both**.* **An earlier version wrote "the margin summand", singular, and put the second copy into $H_r$**, under which a unit vector in that copy has $w=1$ while $c(H)=c_p=\Theta(1/n)$ against a claimed $\Theta(1)$: *wrong by a factor $n$ on a subspace of dimension $\Theta(n^2)$.* [Lemma L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects), [r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) and [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) all say *two*; reading $w$ as the weight outside both restores every number unchanged.

**The two inputs, uniform in $w$, for $m\ge6$**, *$m\ge5$ was overstated: at $n=10$, $\theta_0=6/5<\sqrt{3/2}$, so $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert$ is negative and $\rho^{\mathrm{eff}}$ has no definition.* *[L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) prices **one** $S^2_0(\mathbb R^m)$ copy and is applied here to the **joint** two-copy eigenspace; no $\sqrt2$ is owed, the two images being Hilbert–Schmidt orthogonal and the cross-plane branch reaching only $\sqrt{m-2}/m=0.333$ against $1/\sqrt{m-1}=0.447$ at $n=12$ ([r716](../reviews/REVIEW_r716.md)), recorded because the step was silent.*

- $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le\lVert H_s\rVert_{op}+\lVert H_r\rVert_{op}\le\Theta(n^{-1/2})+w$, by the triangle inequality and [Lemma L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects), which is homogeneous so $\lVert H_s\rVert_F<1$ only helps.
- $c(H)\ge c_p+3\theta_0\tfrac{m-4}{2(m-1)}\,w^2$, the margin being $3\theta_0(\tfrac12-\lambda)$ on each summand of [Lemma L27](nondegeneracy.md) and the form quadratic, so on $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$ it is a **convex combination** of the summand margins. *The smallest off-summand margin is $3\theta_0\tfrac{m-3}{2(m-1)}$ and the coefficient here is that **minus $c_p$**, which is why the two fractions differ by one in the numerator; three readers across two rounds have raised the apparent clash and withdrawn it, so the relation is stated rather than left to be rediscovered.* *The bound once written here, $c(H)\gtrsim c_p+w^2$, is **a lower bound stated upward** at the dimensions that matter: the coefficient is $0.7416$ at $n=12$ and $0.9463$ at $n=14$, reaching $1$ only from $n=16$ ([r716](../reviews/REVIEW_r716.md); reproduced here at six dimensions). **It is unsafe exactly on $n\in\{12,14,16\}$, *$\rho^{\text{eff}}_{16}=\sqrt6-\tfrac{\sqrt{105}}7<1$ precisely because $6>\tfrac{289}{49}$***, and repairing it lowers branch 2 there, which strengthens rather than weakens [L158](#lemma-l158-the-branch-comparison-turns-on-one-dimension-dependent-constant-and-it-says-suplvert-prvert-must-grow-before-i31-can-bind)'s conclusion that branch 2 binds.* **The range matters**: the smallest off-summand margin is the $+\mu$ one, $3\theta_0\frac{m-3}{2(m-1)}$, which is **zero at $m=3$** and ties with $c_p$ at $m=4$, reaching $\ge\tfrac34\theta_0$ only from $m=5$. *The orbit summand is excluded cleanly, $E(\tfrac12)$ **equals** the orbit tangent by [Lemma L27](nondegeneracy.md).*

Hence $\lVert A''\rVert=O(1/\rho)=O(n^{1/2}+nw)$, and with $S:=\sup\lvert P\rvert=\Theta(n^\beta)$,

$$r(H)\;\gtrsim\;\min\!\left(\frac{c_p+u^2}{S+au},\ \frac{\sqrt{c_p+\rho^{\text{eff}}_nw^2}}{n^{1/2}+nw},\ \frac{\rho(H)}2\right),$$

*branch 1 in [L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1)'s form: $u$ is the defect's linear rate and is **not** $w$, the only relation on file being $u\le w$. The display once written here, $\tfrac{c_p+w^2}{S}$, drops $au$ and so **overstates**, the two infima bracket within a bounded factor, so the correction is a constant and not an order ([ROUND_L147](../verification/ROUND_L147.md)).*

> **LEMMA L149.** *For $m\ge6$, the second branch is $\Omega(n^{-1})$ **uniformly in $w\in[0,1]$**, the first is minimised at $w=0$, ***this clause does not follow and is owed***: [L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) pins the **variable** $u$ to zero on the named copy, not the **coefficient** $a$, and at $u=0$ with $a>0$ the function $f$ is *decreasing*, so $w=0$ is where branch 1 is largest rather than smallest. *Three verifiers, [ROUND_L148](../verification/ROUND_L148.md)*, and the withdrawal of this finding recorded in [ROUND_L147](../verification/ROUND_L147.md) is itself withdrawn, and the validity branch is $\Omega(n^{-1})$ as well.*
> **THE CONCLUSION THIS BOX DRAWS IS OWED RATHER THAN REFUTED.** *A refutation recorded here is withdrawn: $r_n\gtrsim n^{-1-\beta}$ cannot be falsified by a smaller lower bound, every branch of the $\min$ bounding $r(H)$ below.* **What holds is that the box asserts the conclusion without the joint minimisation that would establish it**, and that [ROUND_L149-F](../verification/ROUND_L149F.md)'s F12 is right that $u=0$ is not branch 1's minimum, $f'(0)=-ac_p/S^2<0$. *The minimisation of $(c_p+w^2)/(S+n\max(n^{-1/2},w)w)$ is reported by one reader as $\Theta(n^{-1-\beta})$ attained at $w=0$, which would pay the debt rather than refute the claim; it is unadjudicated.* **[L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports)'s $r_n=\Omega(n^{-3/2})$ stands on its own proof and is unaffected either way.**
> $$r_n\;\gtrsim\;n^{-3/2}\quad\text{over every direction},\qquad\text{with }\Theta(n^{-1})\text{ the ceiling of the method,}$$
> *which is [L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports)'s $\Theta(n^{-3/2})$ and is untouched by this correction. Two adjudicators on identical briefs, [ROUND_L149-F](../verification/ROUND_L149F.md) F12.*

> **THE MARGIN COEFFICIENT.** The margin is a quadratic form, so on $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$ it is a **convex combination** of the summand margins and its coefficient is the *smallest surviving* one, the $\Lambda^2\!\otimes\!\Lambda^2$ summand, attained, with $\langle W\rangle$'s negative margin and the orbit's zero margin excluded exactly by $H\perp W^*$ and $H\perp T_{W^*}\mathcal O$. **The penalty lands on that same summand**, so the coefficient the chain carries is
> $$\rho_n^{\mathrm{eff}}=3\theta_0\frac{n-8}{2(n-2)}-\frac29\lvert\kappa_n\rvert,\qquad\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=9\big(\theta_0(n)-\sqrt{3/2}\big),$$
> **increasing from $0.71904$ at $n=12$ to $\tfrac32\sqrt2-2(\sqrt2-\sqrt{3/2})=1.74238$.** > **THE TWO FORMS ARE NOT THE SAME QUANTITY, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS EXACTLY THE PENALTY.** *The bullet's $m$-form $3\theta_0\tfrac{m-4}{2(m-1)}$ is $c_{\min}-c_p$ and nothing else; the $n$-form above is that **minus** $\tfrac29\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=2(\theta_0(n)-\sqrt{3/2})$.* Under $n=2m$ the first term of the $n$-form **is** the $m$-form, $3\theta_0\tfrac{n-8}{2(n-2)}=3\theta_0\tfrac{m-4}{2(m-1)}$, *so the two agree only where the penalty vanishes, which is nowhere above the crossover.* **The gap is $3.04\%$ at $n=12$, $7.81\%$ at $n=14$, $10.22\%$ at $n=16$ and $17.86\%$ in the limit**, the $m$-form tending to $\tfrac32\sqrt2=2.12132$ against the $n$-form's $\sqrt6-\tfrac{\sqrt2}2=1.74238$. **The clause once here, that both equal $c_{\min}-c_p$, would licence substituting either for the other**, and they are consumed in different places, the margin bullet using the unpenalised $m$-form and branch 2 using the penalised $\rho^{\mathrm{eff}}_n$. *The closed form $\sqrt6-\theta_0(n)\tfrac{n+16}{2(n-2)}$ in the box below is the $n$-form and is correct; it reproduces $0.71904$ at $n=12$ and the limit exactly.*

> **POSITIVITY AND MONOTONICITY, WHICH THIS BLOCK CONSUMED AND NEVER PROVED.** *Both follow from the closed form $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n=\sqrt6-\theta_0(n)\tfrac{n+16}{2(n-2)}$ with $\theta_0(n)=\sqrt{2(n-1)(n-2)}/n$, and **monotonicity comes first**, after which positivity costs one integer comparison.* **Monotonicity:** the subtracted term is $p(n)=\tfrac{n+16}{6n}\cdot\kappa$, a product of two positive decreasing factors, so $p$ decreases and $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n=\sqrt6-p(n)$ **strictly increases**, giving $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n\ge\rho^{\text{eff}}_{12}$, which is what [the uniformity proof below](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) consumes. **Positivity** is then $\rho^{\text{eff}}_{12}=\sqrt6-\tfrac7{30}\sqrt{55}>0$, i.e. $6>\tfrac{49\cdot55}{900}$, i.e. $5400>2695$. *Equivalently as a cubic, $(n-1)(n+16)^2<12n^2(n-2)$, which is $11n^3-55n^2-224n+256>0$, worth $8656$ at $n=12$.* **This matters because positivity alone separates the exponents**: at $\rho=0$ the block's own $g_\rho$ gives $\Theta(n^{-3/2})$ at $w=1$, and bounded below it gives $\Theta(n^{-1})$. *Gate 2: [r721](../reviews/REVIEW_r721.md) and [r722](../reviews/REVIEW_r722.md), identical briefs, agreeing; the monotonicity argument is [v14](../verification/VERIFY_v14.md)'s, which had it on file outside this block.*

> **AND BOTH ARE CONDITIONAL ON THE $\tfrac29$, WHICH REVERSES THEM.** *At penalty coefficient $1$ the record's own values run $0.6400\to0.4161$, **decreasing**, so monotonicity is false under that reading rather than merely unproved.* Positivity survives either way. *The $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=9(\theta_0-\sqrt{3/2})$ used here carries its sign only for $n\ge12$.*

> **THE $\tfrac29$ IS DISCHARGED, AND IT IS AN IDENTITY RATHER THAN A BOUND.** *What was owed was the identification of the $(2,2)$ four-set block's $+\mu$ line with $\Lambda^2\!\otimes\!\Lambda^2$, and the record held every ingredient without joining them.* **In [L133](#lemma-l133-the-balanced-orbit-stability-threshold-in-closed-form-and-it-clears-kappa_n-for-every-nge12) step 3's signed coordinates a $(2,2)$ four-set carries $(x,y,z)$ with Bianchi $x+y+z=0$**, so the block is the plane $\{x+y+z=0\}$ and *the coordinate line $\mathbb R(1,0,0)$ meets $\mathcal W_n$ only at $0$*: the object is its projection $X_{ijkl}=(1,0,0)-\tfrac13(1,1,1)=\tfrac13(2,-1,-1)$, which is [L26](nondegeneracy.md)'s $X$ and L133 step 3's $+\mu$ eigenvector at once. **There are $\binom m2^2$ of them, one per four-set and independent**, while [L27](nondegeneracy.md) gives $\dim E_{L_e}(\mu)=\binom m2^2$ with $\mu$ separated from the other nine $L_e$ values on a multiplicity-free list at $m\ge5$, *equal dimensions inside a containment force equality*, so $E_{L_e}(\mu)=\Lambda^2\!\otimes\!\Lambda^2=\bigoplus_{(2,2)}\mathbb RX_{ijkl}$. **Hence $\sigma=x^2/(3(x^2+y^2+z^2))\le\tfrac29$ on all of $\mathcal W_n$ with equality exactly there**, and $\lVert D(\mathrm{Alt}\,W_-)_{W^*}[H]\rVert^2=\tfrac29\lVert\Pi_{\Lambda^2\otimes\Lambda^2}H\rVert^2$ is an identity, $\gamma$ and $\beta$ cancelling. **Gate 2: [r725](../reviews/REVIEW_r725.md) and [r726](../reviews/REVIEW_r726.md), identical briefs, reaching it by different routes.** *So the $\tfrac29$ is attained rather than merely valid, margin and penalty sit on one summand, [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s convex combination has the form it claims, and $\rho^{\mathrm{eff}}_n$ with its monotonicity stands as written.* **The coefficient-$1$ reading is excluded rather than disfavoured**; where it was recorded the constant reads $0.689771$ and decreases to a floor of $0.589804$, the previously recorded $0.582938$ having held $\sqrt2n/(3\sqrt{(n-1)(n-2)})$ at its $n=12$ value inside an $n\to\infty$ limit.

> **HYPOTHESES THE CHAIN USES.** $H\perp W^*$ and $H\perp T_{W^*}\mathcal O$, which the domain $T_{W^*}S\cap T_{W^*}\mathcal O^\perp$ supplies; and **$\ker W^*=0$**, without which $W\mapsto W_-$ is not analytic at $W^*$ and branches 2 and 3 have no domain, it **holds**, the gap being $g_n=\sqrt{2(n-2)}/(n\sqrt{n-1})$ recorded [above](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports). *$\beta$ enters branch 1 and the final display only, not the branch-2 claim.*

**Proof of the uniformity.** Write $g_\rho(w)=\sqrt{c_p+\rho w^2}\big/\big(a_0n^{1/2}+nw\big)$ with $a_0=\sqrt{2n/(n-2)}$ from [Lemma L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects), $c_p=\kappa/n$ and $\rho=\rho^{\mathrm{eff}}_n$. **Here $\kappa:=nc_p=3\theta_0n/(n-2)=3\sqrt{2(n-1)/(n-2)}=4.4497$ at $n=12$**, *distinct from the $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=9(\theta_0-\sqrt{3/2})=0.1016$ three lines above, which it exceeds by a factor $43.8$ and from which one subscript separates it.* **Substituting the wrong one returns $0.19994$ where $0.71980$ belongs, and the signed $\kappa_{12}$ makes the radicand negative**; the record has twice documented readers misled by this. **No case analysis on the location of the stationary point is needed**, since

$$\inf_{w\in[0,1]}g_\rho\;\ge\;\inf_{w\ge0}g_\rho\;=\;\frac1n\sqrt{\frac{\rho\,\kappa}{\rho\,a_0^2+\kappa}},$$

whose $n$-free coefficient $\big(\tfrac{\sqrt2 n}{3\sqrt{(n-1)(n-2)}}+\tfrac1\rho\big)^{-1/2}$ **increases** in $n$, *the displayed quantity itself falls like $1/n$, as it must*, and equals $0.7197978$ at $n=12$, its minimum over even $n\ge12$. **Hence $g_\rho\ge0.71979/n$ uniformly in $w\in[0,1]$.** $\square$

> **THE NUMERAL PRICES $g_\rho$, NOT BRANCH 2.** Branch 2 is $K\,g_\rho$ with $K=\lambda(1-\eta^*)^2/(\sqrt2 J)$ from [L158](#lemma-l158-the-branch-comparison-turns-on-one-dimension-dependent-constant-and-it-says-suplvert-prvert-must-grow-before-i31-can-bind), and $K<1$ by better than an order of magnitude. *The order $\Omega(n^{-1})$ carries through, $K$ being bounded below by a positive constant; the numeral does not, and must not be read as a branch-2 floor, least of all where [L158](#lemma-l158-the-branch-comparison-turns-on-one-dimension-dependent-constant-and-it-says-suplvert-prvert-must-grow-before-i31-can-bind) compares branch infima as a ratio.*

*Round [ROUND_L149](../verification/ROUND_L149.md), verdict `GAP` on the previous proof, repaired here; the repair is itself unaudited.*

**$\Omega$, not $\Theta$, and the reason is [Lemma L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) eight lines above.** At $w=0$ the defect vanishes identically, so the branch is **unbounded** there rather than $\Theta(n^{-1})$. *That slack is the live resource for raising the ceiling, and it is why the chain is an inequality throughout.*

**The penalty term, which had no account.** $-\kappa_n\delta^2$ contributes $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert u^2t^2$ **against** the margin. Since $A'(0)$ is linear and vanishes on the margin subspace by [Lemma L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand), $u\le w$, **and the $\tfrac29$ identity sharpens that to $u=\tfrac{\sqrt2}3\lVert\Pi H\rVert\le0.4714\,w$**, and the net coefficient is $\sqrt6-\tfrac{\sqrt2}2=1.74238>0$. *The $\tfrac32\theta_0-\lvert\kappa_n\rvert\to0.416$ recorded here was the **coefficient-$1$** reading the discharge excludes, a factor $4.19$ low, and its "positive by $20\%$" holds at no dimension, $86.3\%$ at $n=12$ under coefficient $1$, $96.9\%$ under $\tfrac29$.* **The conclusion survives, the true coefficient being larger**, on an inequality that was on no one's record ([r453](../reviews/REVIEW_r453.md)).

> **WHAT THE TUBE DOES NOT DO, RECORDED AT LAST.** *[r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md)'s F6 has been omitted by three entries running.* At either exponent the tube **contains no competitor** and covers a **vanishing fraction** of the sphere, and $\Theta(n^{-1})=\Theta(g_n)$ now puts it at the order of the analyticity radius itself. **The ceiling is unconditional**, $c_p/S\le c_p/\theta_0\le3/(n-2)$, *and a ceiling on a statement this local is a limit on the method rather than on the problem.*

#### Lemma L157, the reach parameter is determined by a fixed point, not chosen: $\eta^*=\tfrac{2t+1-\sqrt{4t+1}}{2t}$

*Derived 2026-09-05 from [r708](../reviews/REVIEW_r708.md)'s crossing condition. **Gate 2: [r709](../reviews/REVIEW_r709.md) and [r710](../reviews/REVIEW_r710.md), identical briefs, agreeing. The entry as first written claimed far more than this and is cut back to what survives.***

**The parameter is $\eta$, and the record has always had it.** [r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) D7 defines the validity branch by exactly the coupling this entry rediscovered, $\lVert A''(s)\rVert\le2M/(\rho-\lvert s\rvert)^2$, hence $t\le\rho/2$, and [line 1285 above](#lemma-l151-withdrawn-it-froze-a-free-parameter-at-an-inadmissible-value-and-called-the-remainder-bounded) carries it as *"the already-frozen $\eta=\tfrac12$"* with its constant $8$. **The first version of this lemma said the record had no definition for it; that was a search by vocabulary rather than by object**, [r708](../reviews/REVIEW_r708.md)'s narrower and true observation, that the *string* $\sigma$ occurs once, widened into a false one about the *quantity*. *[Lemma L150](#lemma-l150-withdrawn-it-is-post-f50s-chain-with-post-f50s-surviving-term-deleted)'s box records this exact failure mode.*

**WHAT IS NEW, AND BOTH REVIEWERS REACH IT INDEPENDENTLY: $\eta$ is not free, it is determined.** The bound on $\lVert A''\rVert$ must hold along the whole segment $[0,r]$, so $\eta=r/\rho$ and the branch is a **self-consistency condition** rather than a family indexed by a parameter:

$$r\;\le\;\frac{\sqrt c}{\sup_{\lvert s\rvert\le r}\lVert A''(s)\rVert}\ .$$

*Its largest solution is the crossing root below.* **[r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) D7 and [r451](../reviews/REVIEW_r451.md) A4 both declared this fixed point absent, calling the branch a cap; it exists, and that is the entry's one contribution.**

> **LEMMA L157.** *In units of $\rho$ the two coupled branches are $\eta$ and $t(1-\eta)^2$ with $t=\lambda\sqrt{c_p+\rho^{\text{eff}}_nw^2}/(2J\gamma)$; they cross at the unique root in $(0,1)$*
> $$\eta^*(t)=\frac{2t+1-\sqrt{4t+1}}{2t},$$
> *the other root being its reciprocal and spurious, and the minimum of the two is maximised there. The exact identity at the crossing is*
> $$\eta^*\gamma=\frac{\lambda\sqrt{c_p+\rho^{\text{eff}}_nw^2}}{2J}\,(1-\eta^*)^2 .$$

**The $(1-\eta^*)^2$ does not drop.** *An earlier display here wrote the second branch without it and so stated a lower bound upward*, a $13$–$22\%$ overstatement at $n=12$, $20\%$ at the binding direction, and **it does not vanish with $n$**: $\sup_wt\to\tfrac18$ at $w=1$, so $(1-\eta^*)^2\to0.808$ at every dimension. *This is the error [r452](../reviews/REVIEW_r452.md) B8 already charged against [Lemma L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform), repeated.* **And $\gamma$ does not cancel**; what is true is that $J$ is *stationary* at the optimum, so $J^*$ is right to $O(t^2)$.

**Three constants inside $t$, and the record's own value for one.** Branch 2 is $\lambda\sqrt c/\lVert A''\rVert$ with $\lambda=2/\sqrt{\lvert\kappa_n\rvert}=1.53$, from [r450](../reviews/REVIEW_r450.md) D3's factor $2$ and the quartic tail's $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert$ ([r709](../reviews/REVIEW_r709.md)). *Every number moves with it: $t$ from $0.135$ to $0.206$ at the binding direction.* **$S$ and the $v\lesssim w$ constant remain open**, so "no free parameter left" is false.

**What freezing $\eta=\tfrac12$ costs, and it is not a factor $4$.** **The binding direction is settled** at [L158](#lemma-l158-the-branch-comparison-turns-on-one-dimension-dependent-constant-and-it-says-suplvert-prvert-must-grow-before-i31-can-bind) **in [r713](../reviews/REVIEW_r713.md)'s favour, in general rather than at one dimension**: rationalising the root to $1-\eta^*=2/(\sqrt{4t+1}+1)$ gives a log-derivative in $(0,1)$ for every $\lambda,J,\gamma$, so $K$'s decrease can never overturn the rise above the kink and [r714](../reviews/REVIEW_r714.md)'s endpoint is refuted. *$0.829$ is refuted.* **The minimiser is the homogeneity kink $w_0=\frac{n-4}n$, which is $\tfrac23$ at $n=12$, a different object from the $0.5528$ this line once named**, and L158 records that reading the two as one has misled three readers once this lemma's own $w$-dependence of $(1-\eta^*)^2$ is kept inside the minimisation, and **neither is $0.829$**, *which this entry, [r709](../reviews/REVIEW_r709.md) and [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md) all cite.* Across the range where branch 2 exists the loss is $2.9$–$3.4$, the value moving about $3\%$ with the location. *And $\lambda$ here is $2/\sqrt{\lvert\kappa_n\rvert}$ at the **finite** $n$: $\lvert\kappa_{12}\rvert=0.10159$ gives $\lambda(12)=6.275$, not the $1.53$ the $n\to\infty$ value returns ([L158](#lemma-l158-the-branch-comparison-turns-on-one-dimension-dependent-constant-and-it-says-suplvert-prvert-must-grow-before-i31-can-bind)).* **At $w=0$ there is no branch 2 to optimise**, the defect vanishes identically there by [Lemma L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand), so the regime the first version called "the one that matters" is the one where the quantity is absent. *And the reach ratio is $\Theta(n^{1/2})$, a factor $7.24$ at $n=12$, not "two orders in $n$".*

**Two couplings.** Branches 1 and 2 are two regimes of the single $f(u)=(c_p+u^2)/(S+au)$ with $a\propto(1-\eta)^{-2}$, so **branch 1 is not untouched**, the effect is about $5\%$ and runs the safe way. And in [L156](#lemma-l156-the-contour-constant-corrected-and-its-optimum-attained-at-the-equidistant-contour) it is the **Cauchy radius $\gamma-r$**, not $\gamma$, that wants to be $d/2$ ([r709](../reviews/REVIEW_r709.md)).

**One point in the entry's favour that it did not make.** At $n=12$ branch 2 beats branch 1 by about $58\times$, so branch 2 alone decides the radius and its constants are exactly what matter.

#### Lemma L158, the branch comparison turns on one dimension-dependent constant, and it says $\sup\lvert P\rvert$ must GROW before [I31](../plan.md) can bind

*Derived 2026-09-05. **Gate 2: [r711](../reviews/REVIEW_r711.md) and [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md), identical briefs. The mechanism is confirmed; every number the entry first attached to it was wrong, and the conclusion reversed.** Rewritten to what survives.*

**Hypotheses, all used and none previously stated.** $n=2m$ even with $n\ge12$; $W^*=S^m\times S^m$ with $\ker W^*=0$; $H\perp W^*$ and $H\perp T_{W^*}\mathcal O$; $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$; and $w\in[0,1]$. *$\lambda=2/\sqrt{\lvert\kappa_n\rvert}$ has a pole at $n=6+2\sqrt7$, which sits $0.71$ below the smallest admissible dimension, **that proximity is why $\lambda(12)$ is four times $\lambda(\infty)$, and why the threshold is $18$ here and $\approx35$ in the limit.*** At $\ker W\ne0$ the entry asserts nothing, [L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1) recording that branches 2 and 3 do not exist there.

**The surviving mechanism.** With [Lemma L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s $c_p=\kappa/n$, branch 1 $=(c_p+w^2)/S$ is increasing in $w$ with infimum $\kappa/(nS)$, and branch 2 has an infimum of order $n^{-1}$. **The $n^{-1}$ cancels from their ratio**, so which branch binds is decided by an inequality carrying no dimension:

$$\frac{\inf_w\text{branch }1}{\inf_w\text{branch }2}=\frac{\sqrt{\kappa(1+\kappa)}}{S\cdot K},\qquad K:=\text{the branch-2 prefactor},$$

*and branch 1 binds, making $S=\sup\lvert P\rvert$, hence [I31](../plan.md), load-bearing, exactly when $S$ exceeds $\sqrt{\kappa(1+\kappa)}/K$.* **$\inf_w\min(f,g)=\min(\inf f,\inf g)$ is an identity, so comparing separately-taken infima is sound**; *the entry's caveat that [Lemma L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) undermines this is withdrawn. L148 changes where the infimum is attained, not its value.*

> **$\kappa$ IS ON THE RECORD AND THE ENTRY SAID IT WAS NOT.** Line 1129 above fixes the margin as $c_p=3\theta_0(\tfrac12-\lambda_m)=3\theta_0/(n-2)$, whence
> $$\kappa=n\,c_p=3\sqrt{\tfrac{2(n-1)}{n-2}},\qquad \kappa^2=\tfrac{18(n-1)}{n-2},\qquad \kappa(12)=4.4497,\qquad \kappa\downarrow3\sqrt2 .$$
> **Both reviewers found it; the entry had searched for the letter $\kappa$ rather than the quantity $nc_p$.** *That is the third vocabulary search this session to invent a gap the record had filled, after [Lemma L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s $\eta$ and [Lemma L150](#lemma-l150-withdrawn-it-is-post-f50s-chain-with-post-f50s-surviving-term-deleted)'s own recorded instance, and it was made after a proposal about exactly this had been filed.*

**THE CONCLUSION REVERSES.** With $\kappa\approx4.45$ the threshold is far above the entry's claimed $\kappa<1.42$, itself an arithmetic slip, the root being $1.48$, so **branch 2 binds at $n=12$ and $S$ does not govern there.** *Both reviewers reach this; it was available from the entry's own ingredients.*

**And $1.5449$ is not $\sup\lvert P\rvert$.** It is the supremum of the **relaxation bound** $\lambda_{\max}p+B_5$. *[bounds.md](bounds.md) records this as a withdrawn framing error twice, [r444](../reviews/REVIEW_r444.md) D6 and [r445](../reviews/REVIEW_r445.md) D1 a third time, and [I31](../plan.md) states it a fourth; this entry was the fourth recurrence.* **The proved bound is [Lemma L143](bounds.md): $\sup\lvert P\rvert\le1+\sqrt{n-2}$, which is $4.162$ at $n=12$.**

> **WHAT THE LEMMA ACTUALLY SAYS, AND IT IS A CONDITION ON [I31](../plan.md) RATHER THAN A VERDICT ON IT.** The known bounds on $S$ are **upper** ones, and an upper bound can only show branch 1 **fails** to bind. So: *branch 1 does not bind wherever $S$'s upper bound falls below the threshold*, which covers $n=12$; and **branch 1 can bind only if $\sup\lvert P\rvert$ genuinely grows past an absolute constant.** Hence **if [I31](../plan.md) succeeds, if $\sup\lvert P\rvert$ is dimension-free and small, branch 1 never binds and branch 2 alone governs the radius at every $n$.** *That is the useful reading, and it is the opposite of the entry's first headline.*

> **THE REFORMULATION [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md) SUPPLIES, AND IT IS THE BEST FRAMING ANY PARTY REACHED.** $\beta$ and $\kappa$ are **not independent**, $\theta_0$ sits in both, since $S\ge\theta_0=\kappa(n-2)/(3n)$. Writing $S=s\,\theta_0(n)$, the criterion becomes
> $$\text{branch 1 binds}\iff s>3.1\text{–}3.6 .$$
> **Under [Conjecture A](../README.md) itself, $s=1$ and it never binds; under the proved upper bound, $s\le3.37$ at $n=12$, and it may.** *So the criterion measures the conjecture-to-proof gap rather than comparing absolute constants, which is what makes it worth having.*

**Three defects in the entry's own algebra, both reviewers.** *Branch 1 is not $(c_p+w^2)/S$*: the chain's own form is $f(u)=(c_p+u^2)/(S+au)$ with $f'(0)<0$ ([L147](#lemma-l147-the-tube-radius-tracks-suplvert-prvert-on-betain0tfrac12-and-the-route-ends-at-thetan-1), [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)), so its infimum is **strictly below** $\kappa/(nS)$ and does not sit at $u=0$. *And $w^*=\kappa n^{-1/2}$ exceeds $1$ for every $n\le19$*, so under the entry's $A=n^{1/2}$ the interior minimum is unattained in most of the range; the repair is the record's $\lVert H_s\rVert_{op}\le1/\sqrt{m-1}$, giving $w^*=3\sqrt{n-1}/n=0.829$, **[L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s own value, which this entry contradicted without noticing**, and the displayed minimum then overstates by $12\%$. *The prefactor $1.24$ is its $w=0$ value, the one direction [L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) empties; at the minimiser it is $1.1075$.*

**Why dropping branch 3 is legitimate, which the entry never said.** [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s $\eta^*$ has already merged it into the prefactor. *Notation: the $\kappa$ here is $nc_p$ and collides with the penalty constant $\kappa_n$ inside the same display; they are unrelated.*

> **SETTLED. GATE 2: [r713](../reviews/REVIEW_r713.md) AND [r714](../reviews/REVIEW_r714.md), AGREEING.** *Tracing the chain: $\lVert A''\rVert\le2M/((1-\eta)\rho)^2$ with $M\le C\rho$ and $\rho=\gamma g_n/\lVert H\rVert_{op}$ gives $\lVert A''\rVert\le2J\lVert H\rVert_{op}\big/\big((1-\eta)^2g_n\big)$, so*
> $$\text{branch }2=\underbrace{\frac{\lambda(1-\eta^*)^2}{\sqrt2\,J}}_{K}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{c_p+\rho^{\text{eff}}_nw^2}}{n\min\big(a_0n^{-1/2}\sqrt{1-w^2}+w,\;1\big)}:\qquad\textbf{$J$ divides $K$.}$$
> **[r711](../reviews/REVIEW_r711.md) was right to carry [L156](#lemma-l156-the-contour-constant-corrected-and-its-optimum-attained-at-the-equidistant-contour)'s $J^*$ and [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md) wrong to omit it**, though r711 used $J^*=\tfrac{40}9$, L156's **out-of-scope $m=2$ endpoint**, where in scope $6.9421\le J^*<8$. *The doubt this entry raised against its own claim, that the $\gamma$ in $\rho$ might not be the $\gamma$ in $J^*$, **fails**: L156 names both in one hypothesis, and $J\gamma=C$ makes [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s $t=\lambda\sqrt{c_p+w^2}/(2C)$ exact rather than small-$t$.*

> **THE THRESHOLD IS PINNED AT $\approx17$–$19$ AT $n=12$.** *The spread between the two audits, $17$–$19$ against $52.92$, is one constant:* **$\lambda=2/\sqrt{\lvert\kappa_n\rvert}$ was evaluated at the $n\to\infty$ value $\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=1.7053$, while the record prints $\lvert\kappa_{12}\rvert=0.10159$ in three places** ([L133](#lemma-l133-the-balanced-orbit-stability-threshold-in-closed-form-and-it-clears-kappa_n-for-every-nge12), [L134](route-a.md), the [ledger](LEDGER.md)). *So $\lambda(12)=6.275$, not $1.53$, a factor $4.1$, and it is **the largest $n$-dependence in a ratio this entry's title calls $n$-free**, moving $2.3\times$ across the range against $\kappa$'s $5\%$.* **Using an asymptotic constant at a finite $n$ is the same class of error as the three vocabulary searches above.**

> **AND THE CONCLUSION IS FIRMER THAN EITHER SIDE FIRST HAD IT.** Against [L143](bounds.md)'s $S\le4.162$ and [L70](bounds.md)'s $S\le10$ at $n=12$, **branch 2 binds at $n=12$ decisively, the threshold there being the number $T=\tfrac{\kappa\sqrt2J}{\lambda(1-\eta^*)^2s_\rho(w_0)}=18.00$ at $\rho^{\text{eff}}$, and $17.26$ at the coefficient $1$.** *Three readers derived $T$ independently and agree to three digits. **The band "$17$–$19$" recorded earlier was $J^*$ read at its $m\to\infty$ endpoint**, the same error this entry charges against $\lambda$ four lines above, and is withdrawn; $52.92$ reproduces from no reading and is withdrawn with it.* **The extension "provably so at every $n$ up to $\approx1800$" is withdrawn**: it had no argument on the page, it reconstructs only at the coefficient $1$, and carrying every constant to the corrected coefficient gives $S^*$ capped at $34.71$ against $1+\sqrt{n-2}$, so the crossing is $n\approx1140$. *That is still far above every dimension this programme evaluates, so the conclusion below is unaffected.* *This reverses [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md)'s "branch 1 binds from $n\ge14$", which was also unsound in kind: an upper bound on $S$ cannot show branch 1 binds.* **So [I31](../plan.md)'s constant is not load-bearing in any dimension this programme reaches**, and in [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md)'s $S=s\theta_0$ variable the threshold is $s^*\approx15$, not $3.1$–$3.6$.

**Four constants the entry had wrong, both reviewers agreeing.** $a_0=\sqrt{2n/(n-2)}=1.549$ at $n=12$ and is **$n$-dependent**, not $1$; the exact $1/g_n=n^2c_p/6$, the $\sqrt2$ limit being $4.9\%$ low **in the unsafe direction**; $\eta^*$ was read at $w=0$, the one direction [L148](#lemma-l148-the-defect-vanishes-identically-on-the-margin-summand) empties of branch 2; and $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le\min\big(\sqrt{2/(n-2)}+w,\,1\big)$ carries a kink the entry ignored. *[r713](../reviews/REVIEW_r713.md) adds that $M\le C\rho$ is sharp only at $\lVert H\rVert_{op}=1$, the record's own bound is $M\le C\gamma g_n$, quadratic in $\lVert H\rVert_{op}$, which is inert at the binding direction but wrong as an identity.*

> **THE MINIMISER IS THE HOMOGENEITY KINK $w_0=\dfrac{n-4}n$ AT $n=12$ AND $n=14$, AND NOT ABOVE**, *the proof inside this box is $n$-local and the box carried no range, which is how [L162](#lemma-l162-the-composed-constant-f-in-closed-form-in-n-and-what-it-is-not) came to import it without one; the criterion that decides it is recorded there.* **AT $n=12$ IT IS $\tfrac23$. GATE 2: [r715](../reviews/REVIEW_r715.md) AND [r716](../reviews/REVIEW_r716.md), AGREEING.** *The premise holds*: $\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le\lVert H\rVert_F=1$ is legitimate, [r716](../reviews/REVIEW_r716.md) re-deriving [L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects)'s normalisation from its own eigenvalue list and confirming $w\in[0,1]$ and $a_0n^{-1/2}=\sqrt{2/(n-2)}=1/\sqrt{m-1}$ as one quantity written two ways. **What was wrong is the envelope.** $H_s\perp H_r$ makes $\lVert H_s\rVert_F=\sqrt{1-w^2}$ an *identity* and [L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) is homogeneous, *[L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) says so in the very bullet that then drops it*, so the admissible envelope is
> $$\lVert H\rVert_{op}\le\min\Big(a_0n^{-1/2}\sqrt{1-w^2}+w,\ 1\Big),\qquad\text{crossing }1\text{ at }w_0=\frac{1-a_0^2/n}{1+a_0^2/n}=\frac{n-4}n,$$
> **never larger than the triangle form, and equal to exactly $1$ at $w=1$**, *so [r714](../reviews/REVIEW_r714.md)'s $1.4472$ prices an $H_s$ that $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$ has already emptied, an objection more elementary than the $\min$ and needing none.*

> **THE $\mathbf{r713}$/$\mathbf{r714}$ DISPUTE IS SETTLED WITHOUT ARITHMETIC.** Rationalising [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s root to $1-\eta^*=2/(\sqrt{4t+1}+1)$ and writing $s=\sqrt{c_p+w^2}$, $t=\beta s$,
> $$\frac{\mathrm d\log\big(K(w)\sqrt{c_p+\rho^{\text{eff}}_nw^2}\big)}{\mathrm d\log s}=\frac1{\sqrt{4t+1}}\in(0,1)\quad\text{for every }\lambda,J,\gamma .$$
> **So $K$'s decrease can never overturn the rise above the kink** ([r715](../reviews/REVIEW_r715.md)), [r714](../reviews/REVIEW_r714.md)'s disputed point is refuted in general rather than at one dimension, and [r713](../reviews/REVIEW_r713.md)'s shape is right.

**THE CONSTANT THIS ENTRY NEVER PRINTS, AND WHOSE ABSENCE PRODUCED THREE FALSE FINDINGS AGAINST CORRECT FIGURES.** *Every numeral below is a value of the envelope-free $G(w)=(1-\eta^*)^2\sqrt{c_p+\rho w^2}=\tfrac{4s}{(q+1)^2}$ with $q=\sqrt{4\beta s+1}$, and the constant that fixes $\beta$ is read off [L156](#lemma-l156-the-contour-constant-corrected-and-its-optimum-attained-at-the-equidistant-contour) rather than stated here:* $C=\tfrac{4+2D}D=\tfrac{42}{11}$ at $n=12$, so $\beta=\lambda/(2C)=\tfrac{11\lambda}{84}$. **The same substitution returns this entry's own printed $J^*=6.9421$, which is what confirms the reading.**

**The kink understates the minimum by $4.9\%$.** *At $n=12$ with $\lambda(12)=6.275$ the minimum was recorded as $0.40347$ at $w_0=\tfrac23$, against $0.38468$ at the entry's $0.5528$. **The two baselines are different objects and are named here because reading them as one has misled three readers.** *$0.5528=1-1/\sqrt5$ is the kink of the **triangle** envelope this entry supersedes, where the current envelope gives $\phi(0.5528)=1-\tfrac{\sqrt5-\sqrt{2\sqrt5-1}}5=0.92546$, so the like-for-like value there is $F(0.5528)=G/\phi=0.41565$, which exceeds $F(w_0)=0.40346$ by $3.02\%$ and points the same way as the $4.9\%$.* **The endpoint is r714's**, its $\psi(1)=1+\sqrt{2/(n-2)}=1.4472$ being the refuted envelope at $w=1$; the improvement over it is $\psi(1)G(w_0)/G(1)-1$, **which increases in $\lambda$**, $27.8\%$ at $\lambda(12)$ and $19.4\%$ at the asymptotic $\lambda$, never the $26\%$ claimed. *The $\rho^{\text{eff}}$ correction leaves r714's $1.4472$ alone, and whether it should is not determined here.* > **THE NUMERAL BELOW IS REFUTED AND OVERSTATED, AND WHAT IT MEASURES IS UNSETTLED.** *It was recorded as a lower bound on the tube radius. **It is $G$**, branch 2 without its $\lambda/(\sqrt2J)$ and $1/n$, which is $0.0206$ at $w_0$, and the numeral itself is exact, three readers reproducing $G(w_0)=0.403463$ independently. **What was wrong is the label, not the value.*** *$t$ and this branch were evaluated with the coefficient $1$ on $w^2$ where [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s repaired numerator carries $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n=2\sqrt{3/2}-\theta_0(n)\tfrac{n+16}{2(n-2)}$, which is $\sqrt6-\tfrac7{30}\sqrt{55}=0.7190434<1$ at $n=12$.* **So the recorded figure is too large by exactly $1.04329$ at the binding $w_0=\tfrac23$, and its corrected value is $0.38673$.** *Both numerals are $(1-\eta^*)^2\sqrt{c_p+\rho w^2}$, **neither is branch 2**, which carries a further $\lambda/(\sqrt2J)$ and $1/n$ and is $0.0206$ at $w_0$. The ratio is what the correction establishes; the base has never been reproduced from the record's constants by any reader who has tried.* *The factor is envelope-free, $n\min(\cdot)$ cancels from $F_1/F_\rho=\tfrac{N_1}{N_\rho}\big(\tfrac{s_\rho+1}{s_1+1}\big)^2$, and since the envelope alone fixes the minimiser, the ratio at $w_0$ is the ratio at the minimum. The $8.667\%$ is the inflation of $t$; the elasticity $1/s$ damps it to $4.33\%$ in the radius.* **The minimiser stays at $w_0=\tfrac{n-4}n$ under $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n$**, and **at $n=12$** the kink is a strict minimum across the whole of $[0,1]$: above $w_0$ the envelope is constant so the derivative is $+\tfrac1s\tfrac{N'}N>0$, below it the envelope's slope is $\phi'(w_0)=\tfrac35$ exactly against $\tfrac1s\tfrac{N'}N\le\tfrac{\sqrt\rho}{2\sqrt{c_p}\,s_{\min}}=0.4019$ uniformly, by AM–GM. **The sufficient condition is $n$-local and is not claimed beyond $n=12$**: $\tfrac1{2\sqrt{c_p}}\sim0.243\sqrt n$ grows while $\phi'(w_0)\downarrow\tfrac12$, so the two cross and the argument stops. *The proviso this carried is discharged: [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s $\tfrac29$ is an identity attained on $E_{L_e}(\mu)$, so the alternative reading that would have moved the factor to $1.0391$ is excluded and $1.04329$ is the value.* **Gate 2: [r719](../reviews/REVIEW_r719.md) and [r720](../reviews/REVIEW_r720.md), identical briefs, agreeing to five digits and reaching $0.38673$ independently.** *The defect is confined to $n\in\{12,14,16\}$, $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n$ is $0.7190$, $0.8724$, $0.9856$, then $1.0727$ at $n=18$, from which the coefficient $1$ is valid and merely loose.* **What survives untouched:** the order claims $r_n\gtrsim n^{-1-\beta}$ and the $\Omega(n^{-1})$ ceiling; [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s own $g_\rho\ge0.71979/n$, whose proof already carries $\rho^{\text{eff}}_n$; and this entry's verdict that branch 2 binds, **which the repair strengthens**, since lowering branch 2 makes it bind more surely. *Adjudicated by two readers on identical briefs, [ROUND_L157](../verification/ROUND_L157.md) D6.*

**The figures $0.2793$ and $0.2209$ reproduce from no combination of the record's constants**, forcing them needs $\lambda=7.48$ and $7.21$, and no single constant yields both.

**$Bc/A=0.82916$ exactly, and it fails twice over.** It lies above **both** candidate kinks, and it minimises only the prefactor-free $g$, *so the value [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t), [r709](../reviews/REVIEW_r709.md) and [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md) cite is unreachable and stationary for the wrong function.* *It is also not this entry's earlier attribution to [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s literal $A=n^{1/2}$, which gives $1.2845$.*

> **TWO DEFECTS IN [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) NEITHER PARTY RAISED, FROM [r716](../reviews/REVIEW_r716.md).** *Its margin bound $c(H)\gtrsim c_p+w^2$ is **a lower bound stated upward**: the margin is a quadratic form, so on $\lVert H\rVert_F=1$ it is a convex combination, $c(H)\ge c_p+3\theta_0\tfrac{m-4}{2(m-1)}w^2$ with coefficient $0.74162$ at $n=12$ and $0.94626$ at $n=14$, reaching $1$ only at $n=16$.* **Repairing it strengthens the case for the homogeneity kink.** *And [L146](#lemma-l146-the-operator-norm-on-the-margin-summand-and-the-direction-blind-radius-it-corrects) prices one $S^2_0$ copy where [L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) applies it to the joint two-copy eigenspace; no $\sqrt2$ is owed, the images being HS-orthogonal and the cross-plane branch reaching only $\sqrt{m-2}/m=0.333$ against $1/\sqrt{m-1}=0.447$, but the record should say so.*

**Branch 2 binds at $n=12$ under every envelope considered, so no reading makes [I31](../plan.md) load-bearing there.**

**THE DISAGREEMENT AS THE REVIEWERS LEFT IT.** The reviewers differ on the threshold's value, hence on where the crossover sits. [r711](../reviews/REVIEW_r711.md) puts it near $21$, carrying $A=3.980$ from $\lVert H_s\rVert_{op}=\sqrt{2/(n-2)}$, $B=1/g_n=0.74n$, and **[L156](#lemma-l156-the-contour-constant-corrected-and-its-optimum-attained-at-the-equidistant-contour)'s $2J^*$ inside $\lVert A''\rVert$, which the entry dropped, but read at $\tfrac{40}9$, the $m=2$ endpoint L156's corollary places outside the programme's range, so the figure it used is $8.89$ where $n=12$ gives $2J^*=\tfrac{1680}{121}=13.884$, a factor $\tfrac{189}{121}$**, and concludes branch 1 cannot bind at $n=12$ since [Lemma L70](bounds.md) caps $S\le n-2=10$. [r712](../reviews/REVIEW_r712.md) puts it near $3.98$–$4.44$ against $S\le4.162$ and calls $n=12$ undecided. *The gap is whether $2J^*$ enters the branch-2 prefactor $K$, and settling it is the next task on this item.* **Both agree the entry's own threshold was wrong and that $\kappa\approx4.45$.**

**A $w$-dependence the entry ignored.** $K$ is not constant: [L157](#lemma-l157-the-reach-parameter-is-determined-by-a-fixed-point-not-chosen-etatfrac2t1-sqrt4t12t)'s $t$ depends on $w$, so $\lambda(1-\eta^*)^2$ ranges over $[1.24,1.53]$, and the entry used the $w=1$ value at an interior minimiser.

#### Lemma L150, WITHDRAWN: it is post F50's chain with post F50's surviving term deleted

*Recorded and withdrawn 2026-09-03. **Gate 2: [r454](../reviews/REVIEW_r454.md) REFUTED it as a route**, the algebra is right and the consequence was already refuted, in this file, 230 lines above where it was written.*

> **[POST F50](#post-f50-the-four-set-concentration-route-the-lemma-is-false-and-both-projections-fail-in-opposite-ways) IS TITLED "THE FOUR-SET CONCENTRATION ROUTE: THE LEMMA IS FALSE".** Gate 2 by r389/r390, corrected at r400, and [Lemma L130](diagonal-half.md)(i) upgrades it to a theorem: **no reduction of the four-set clause to $m_4$ alone can work.** *L150 was exactly that reduction.*

**And it was strictly weaker than the incumbent it duplicated.** [Post F50](#post-f50-the-four-set-concentration-route-the-lemma-is-false-and-both-projections-fail-in-opposite-ways) keeps $\Sigma\le3m_4-\tfrac12\sigma^2$; L150's step $\tfrac12(3S_2-S_1^2)\le\tfrac32S_2$ discards the $-\tfrac12\sigma^2$. *F50 closes with the instruction a successor needs and this one ignored: **use the identity, not the mass alone.*** At every in-band witness the true $\Sigma$ is exactly $0$, so the discarded term carried the whole content.

> **THE SCREEN THAT WOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT WAS A SEARCH, NOT A COMPUTATION.** *The claim "no fence post covers the L121 or L122 blocks" came from grepping the traps index for the strings `L121` and `L122`*, **searching by vocabulary rather than by value**, which is the failure recorded against [Lemma L142](bounds.md) the same day. A post whose title names the route in words the grep did not contain sat in the file being edited.

**Three further defects, each independent.**

- **The norm.** [conventions.md](conventions.md) fixes $\lVert K\rVert_F^2=2$ over ordered pairs, so "$\lVert M\rVert\le\lVert W\rVert=1$" is false as written; only the explicitly unordered sum keeps the stated threshold. *And it cited [I11](../plan.md), an unchecked plan item, where the diagonalising-frame identity $\sum_{a<b}M_{ab}^2=\mathop{\mathrm{tr}}W^2=1$ makes the citation unnecessary.*
- **[Lemma L24](#lemma-l24-second-order-optimality-as-an-eigenvalue-bound-the-canonical-statement) does not apply where [I18](../plan.md) quantifies.** L24 gives $\Sigma\le\theta^2$ at maximisers; [I18](../plan.md) ranges over **pure critical points**, where $\Sigma>\theta^2$ occurs, $S^2\times S^4$ at $\mu=\tfrac23$ gives $\max\Sigma=1.667$ against $\theta^2=0.9375$. *That example is printed in [Lemma L128](#lemma-l128-the-branching-clause-block-checkable-nondegeneracy-at-a-pure-critical-point-and-the-isolation-it-gives)'s own box in this file, recording two reviewers refuting an earlier version of the same confusion.*
- **The saturation caveat was understated to the point of being wrong.** The hollow zero-row-sum $4\times4$ space is $2$-dimensional and **every** element saturates all three steps automatically, its only critical orbit sitting at $\theta=\sqrt3/2$, out of band. *So hollowness and zero row sums carry no concentration content at all*, where the entry called them the unspent hypotheses that would rescue it.

**What does not fall is the clause.** $\Sigma=0$ at every in-band witness, so [I18](../plan.md)'s $\Sigma\ne\theta^2$ holds there comfortably. **[I18](../plan.md) returns to blocked**: [post F50](#post-f50-the-four-set-concentration-route-the-lemma-is-false-and-both-projections-fail-in-opposite-ways) is a live fence across the route this entry proposed, and the item had no named input before it and has none now.

## Post F61, any four-set criterion that drops $S_1$ is false, and $S^5\times S^q$ is the witness

*[r454](../reviews/REVIEW_r454.md) and [r455](../reviews/REVIEW_r455.md), identical briefs, agreeing and each constructing the counterexample independently, [r455](../reviews/REVIEW_r455.md) in exact rationals, from Ricci-flatness alone with the record's values reproduced before anything was believed.*

**The witness.** $S^5\times S^{43}$ at $n=48$ is a pure critical point in band, $\theta^2=1.596764\ge\tfrac32$, with a four-subset carrying $0.501203$ of the Frobenius mass. Along $S^5\times S^q$ as $q\to\infty$, $\theta^2\to\tfrac85$ and the concentration $\to\tfrac35$. *Hollowness, zero row sums, purity, criticality, $n\ge12$ and the band all hold there.*

> **THE FAILURE IS ANTI-ALIGNED WITH THE CLAUSE, WHICH IS WHY MASS ALONE CANNOT WORK.** The offending four-sets are the **within-factor** ones, where $A=B=C=2\alpha$ and $\Sigma=0$ **exactly**. The $(2,2)$-splits, the only four-sets with $\Sigma\ne0$, carry about $0.098$. *A criterion in the mass alone is largest exactly where the quantity it bounds is zero.*

**And the fatal step is the second, not the third.** At a within-factor four-set $3S_2=S_1^2$, so the whole of $\Sigma$ is the cancellation that $\Sigma\le\tfrac32S_2$ discards; the third step $\tfrac32S_2\le3m_4$ is **tight** there. *This locates [post F50](INDEX-traps.md)'s "use the identity, not the mass alone" precisely: the identity's $-\tfrac12\sigma^2$ is the entire content at every witness.*

**What a successor must clear.** A four-set criterion must keep $S_1$. *[r455](../reviews/REVIEW_r455.md) offers one that does, $6\sigma_S-S_1(S)^2<2\theta^2$ at every four-subset, which passes at the $n=48$ counterexample with **zero violations across the in-band two-factor family to $n=600$**.* **That is a screen and not a proof**, and it is a candidate for [I18](../plan.md) rather than a result.

**One correction it forces elsewhere.** At a pure critical point $\sum_{a<b}M_{ab}^2=1$ is an **equality** from [conventions.md](conventions.md), so the citation of [I11](../plan.md), an unchecked plan item, was never needed for this or for anything shaped like it.

#### Lemma L151, WITHDRAWN: it froze a free parameter at an inadmissible value and called the remainder bounded

*Recorded and withdrawn 2026-09-03. **Gate 2: [r457](../reviews/REVIEW_r457.md) REFUTED it**, deriving the contour family by hand.*

> **THE CONSTANT IS NOT A CONSTANT; IT IS A FUNCTION OF THE PARAMETER THE ENTRY FROZE.** With $\Gamma$ the circle of radius $\alpha g_n$ about $\lambda_-$ and $\rho=\gamma g_n/\lVert H\rVert_{op}$,
> $$M\le C(\alpha,\gamma)\,\rho,\qquad C(\alpha,\gamma)=\frac{\alpha+\tfrac m{m-1}}{\alpha-\gamma}.$$
> **At the entry's own values $\alpha=\gamma=\tfrac12$ this is $+\infty$.** *So its two "pinned" rows, the contour's $\tfrac12$ and the owed $C$, were one free parameter counted twice, fixed at an inadmissible point, with the remainder called "one bounded computation with every input already fixed".*

**And $\tfrac12$ was never forced.** Optimising the defect branch gives $\gamma=\alpha/2$, *half the **contour radius**, not half the gap*, with the supremum at $\alpha\to1$, unattained. **The entry called it "pinned by the geometry, not chosen".**

> **THIS IS NOT NEW: [Lemma L145](#lemma-l145-the-constant-chain-for-the-balanced-orbit-tube-radius-and-the-lower-bound-r_nomegan-32-it-supports)'s own table reads *"the complex radius must be $\rho<g_n/2$, **not** $\rho=g_n/2$"*, and [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md), [r447](../reviews/REVIEW_r447.md) and [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md) each corrected it before. *The entry re-asserted $\rho=g_n/2$ as pinned.*

**The list of four was not complete either.** At $\beta=0$ **all three** branches of [Lemma L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform)'s chain are $\Theta(n^{-1})$, and branch $1$, $c_p/S$, contains no Cauchy constant at all, *so a minimum over three branches cannot turn on $C$ alone.* **At least nine constants enter**, including $\tfrac32\theta_0-\lvert\kappa_n\rvert=0.4161$, which is a **positivity condition rather than a scale**, the $c_0\ge\tfrac34\theta_0$ that carries the $m\ge5$ range, and the $u\lesssim w$ dependency whose constant is unknown under the route [Lemma L149](#lemma-l149-no-direction-is-both-low-margin-and-high-operator-norm-so-the-bound-is-uniform) actually cites.

**The two rows whose algebra was right were base-point facts used along the segment.** Cauchy's $k!=2$ becomes $2M/((1-\eta)\rho)^2$ uniformly, hence **$8$** at the already-frozen $\eta=\tfrac12$; and the Leibniz term $2\langle A''',A\rangle$ vanishes at $t=0$ **only**, contributing at the same order for $s\asymp\rho$. *That is the error the entry named correctly in its own $C=1$ paragraph and committed one row above it.*

**It was a restatement, not a reduction.** $M=O(\rho)$ with finite $C$ on the complex circle is **already proved**, by four reviewers, so only a value was ever owed and not an order; the estimate it "named as owed" is the one [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md) and [r446](../reviews/REVIEW_r446.md) had both ordered written. *And its paragraph naming the remaining work omitted $\lVert\Pi(z)\rVert_{op}=\alpha/(\alpha-\gamma)$, the factor that diverges, which [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md) had warned is not $\le1$ off the Hermitian locus.*

> **AND THE CONSTANT IT CALLED OWED WAS ALREADY ON THE RECORD.** [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md) computes $M\le6\rho$ ([r456](../reviews/REVIEW_r456.md)). *So the entry's one claimed contribution, isolating a single unpinned constant, named a constant that had a value.* **This is the fourth novelty failure of the day**, after [Lemma L142](bounds.md), [Lemma L150](#lemma-l150-withdrawn-it-is-post-f50s-chain-with-post-f50s-surviving-term-deleted) and the $\rho<g_n/2$ correction above.

> **WHAT SURVIVES AND IS WORTH KEEPING.** *$C=1$ on the real segment holds in full*, including $\lVert\mathrm{Alt}\rVert_{op}\le1$, [conventions.md](conventions.md) states $\lVert\mathrm{Alt}(X)\rVert\le\lVert X\rVert$ directly, and the $\tfrac13$ sits inside the projector formula, being what makes it idempotent. **And the contour family above is the real result of this entry**: it is the object a successor optimises, and it came from the reviewer rather than the lemma.

#### The contour constant for the balanced-orbit defect, and a live disagreement in it

*Not a lemma of this record's own derivation: the object below came from [r456](../reviews/REVIEW_r456.md) and [r457](../reviews/REVIEW_r457.md), each deriving it by hand while refuting [Lemma L151](#lemma-l151-withdrawn-it-froze-a-free-parameter-at-an-inadmissible-value-and-called-the-remainder-bounded), and from [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md), which had computed a value for it earlier. **It is recorded here because it is what a successor optimises, and because the two derivations disagree.***

**The set-up both use.** Take $\Gamma$ the circle of radius $\alpha g_n$ about $\lambda_-$ and the disc radius $\rho=\gamma g_n/\lVert H\rVert_{op}$. Then $M=\sup_{\lvert z\rvert=\rho}\lVert A\rVert\le C(\alpha,\gamma)\,\rho$ with

| source | $C(\alpha,\gamma)$ |
|---|---|
| [r456](../reviews/REVIEW_r456.md) | $\dfrac{1+\alpha}{\alpha-\gamma}$ |
| [r457](../reviews/REVIEW_r457.md) | $\dfrac{\alpha+\tfrac m{m-1}}{\alpha-\gamma}$ |

> **SETTLED FOR [r456](../reviews/REVIEW_r456.md) BY [LEMMA L152](#lemma-l152-refuted-as-stated-the-numerator-identity-holds-the-constant-it-was-built-into-has-no-range-and-the-disagreement-it-claimed-to-resolve-was-never-one), GATE 2 OWED.** *The numerator is $\max_\Gamma\lvert\zeta\rvert$ on a circle about $\lambda_-$, hence $(1+\alpha)g_n$; $\lambda_+$ constrains the admissible $\alpha$ without entering it.* **The two agree as $m\to\infty$, which is why the error was invisible at large $n$ and worth $20\%$ at $n=12$.**

**What is agreed, and it is most of it.**

- **$\alpha=\gamma$ is inadmissible**: the Neumann ratio is $1$ there and $C$ diverges. *[Lemma L151](#lemma-l151-withdrawn-it-froze-a-free-parameter-at-an-inadmissible-value-and-called-the-remainder-bounded) froze both at $\tfrac12$.*
- **The $\tfrac12$ was never forced.** $0\notin\mathop{\mathrm{spec}}(W^*)$ and $\lambda_+=\tfrac m{m-1}g_n$, so separating the negative part from $0$ constrains nothing; $\alpha$ may exceed $1$.
